([kyubiful/dotfiles](https://github.com/kyubiful/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/opencode/agents/AIDevRetro.md))
- **AIDevTechPlanner**: Expert technical architect and tech-lead for the Inditex ecosystem. ([kyubiful/dotfiles](https://github.com/kyubiful/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/opencode/agents/AIDevTester.md))
- **AIDevVerifier**: Standard verification agent.
